## Dependency Cube Mode

Work now uses dependency cubes for prioritization without changing the verification contract. Each cube groups source Java, Godot runtime wiring, and playable behavior for one gameplay system. Inside a cube, every slice still moves linearly: read upstream Java, write RED tests, prove RED, implement minimal GREEN, run targeted/full tests, prove through Godot MCP runtime, update this checklist, run `git diff --check`, stage only touched tracked files, and commit.

Current cube: `Unit weapons / bullet creation lifecycle`. Current source path is `BulletType.java`, continuing `BulletType.create(...)` internals after the source-backed `Weapon.bullet(...)` chain reached `bullet.create(...)` with bullet initial guard, geometry, angle/lifetime scale, shooter selection, create call argument, handle call dispatch, handleBullet continuous geometry, handleBullet aim endpoint update, bullet sound branch, bullet effects branch, bullet recoil velocity, bullet shake effect, bullet mount recoil, bullet recoil slots, heat, BulletType initial gate, spawnUnit branch, normal bullet identity, aim target, velocity/position, lifetime/payload/damage, trail reset/add, keep velocity/return, init initial kill/instant-disappear, init spawn-bullets, update dispatch, update interval-bullet, update trail, update homing initial gate/real aim, update homing target velocity, update homing follow aim, update weaving slices, update trail-effects slices, init type defaults, init frag velocity, init lightning type, init light radius, init draw size, init range assignment, calculateRange override, calculateRange spawnUnit, calculateRange despawnUnit, calculateRange fallback, continuousDamage default, heals, testCollision, buildingDamage, shieldDamage, draw dispatch, drawTrail, drawLight, drawParts, hit default/x-y/effects/frag/post-frag/suppression/splash-dispatch, createSplashDamage damage/status/heal/makeFire dispatch, createFrags initial guard/loop boundary/random length/angle geometry/create call/frags increment, createPuddles initial gate/loop boundary/tileWorld random geometry/deposit, createIncend chance gate/call, createUnits initial gate/loop boundary/random offset/spawn call/rotation/notify, despawned hit/createUnits dispatch, despawned effect call, despawned sound call, despawned shake call, removed trail fade, removed frag dispatch, and source-symbol/API/schema registration for the existing buildingDamage, shieldDamage, and draw dispatch states.

## Scope

This project does not attempt a blind one-shot conversion of all Java source into working Godot gameplay. It builds a repeatable transpiler pipeline that gradually converts Mindustry source into a Godot 4.6.2 GDScript project while preserving traceability back to upstream files.

The first milestone is a Godot project that loads without parse errors and contains generated GDScript for a small content subset. Gameplay accuracy comes after the generator, runtime shims, and asset bridge are stable.

## Non-Negotiable Accuracy Rules

1. No item moves from `Not Done` to `Done` without fresh verification output.
2. No generated source is called valid until Godot parses it.
3. No parser feature is called supported until it has at least one fixture test.
4. No new source-backed or explicit-input variable is accepted without a `source_symbol_registry.json` entry that names its upstream Java source, method, expression, Godot owner, tests, and MCP proof.
5. Before adding a new `source_*` migration owner, check `source_symbol_registry.json`, `source_symbol_audit_report.json`, this checklist, and the target upstream Java. Add or update registry symbols first, regenerate the audit report after registry changes, and keep the new owner out of the legacy-unregistered backlog.
6. Resumed work after context reset must reload the same SSOT files before editing: `godot-mindustry/src/generated/source_symbol_registry.json`, `godot-mindustry/src/generated/source_symbol_audit_report.json`, this design checklist, and the exact upstream Java file/method for the slice.
7. New registry entries must carry canonical naming metadata: exact `java_expr`, canonical `godot_symbol`, `godot_owner`, `status` (`source-backed`, `explicit-input`, `deferred`, or `generated`), tests, MCP runtime proof, and rule metadata. If a legacy symbol name must remain, record `alias_of` or an audit note before touching that subsystem.
8. New GDScript input and local variable names use snake_case. Runtime output/API keys may keep Java camelCase only when the registry marks them canonical or aliases them to a canonical symbol. Do not invent synonym variables when a canonical symbol already exists.
9. Do not perform broad legacy renaming while migrating gameplay. Clean inconsistent legacy names only inside the touched subsystem unless an explicit cleanup slice says otherwise.
10. No unsupported Java construct may disappear silently. It must appear in a report with file path and line number.
11. No manual gameplay rewrite is mixed into generated files. Handwritten Godot runtime code lives separately from generated output.
12. GPL-3.0 source lineage must be preserved. Generated Godot derivative must keep license and modified-source notice.

## Architecture

### 1. Source Scanner

The scanner walks `mindustry-upstream/core/src` and records source facts into a manifest. It does not transform code. It records packages, imports, top-level classes, enums, interfaces, inheritance, and file hashes.

Output example:

```json
{
  "source_root": "mindustry-upstream/core/src",
  "files": [
    {
      "path": "core/src/mindustry/content/Items.java",
      "package": "mindustry.content",
      "types": ["Items"],
      "sha256": "computed_file_hash"
    }
  ]
}
```

### 2. Java AST Parser

The parser must use a real Java parser. Preferred options are `tree-sitter-java` in Node/Python or JavaParser through a small Java CLI. Regex-only parsing is forbidden for class/method bodies.

Required extracted nodes:

- class declarations
- enum declarations
- fields
- constructors
- methods
- static initializers
- imports
- inheritance and interfaces
- method calls
- object creation
- lambda and anonymous-class unsupported reports

### 3. Godot Emitter

The emitter converts parsed Java AST into GDScript files under `res://src/mindustry/<package_path>/`.

Mapping rules:

| Java | GDScript |
| --- | --- |
| `package mindustry.content` | `src/mindustry/content/` folder |
| `public class Items` | `class_name Items` |
| constructor | `_init` with matching parameter list |
| static field | class-level `static var` when legal, otherwise registry singleton |
| method overload | suffix with type/count hash, then add dispatcher if needed |
| enum | generated constants plus lookup dictionary |
| Java arrays/list literals | `Array` or typed compatibility wrapper |
| Arc `Seq` | handwritten runtime `Seq.gd` |

Generated files include a header:

```gdscript
# Generated from Mindustry upstream.
# Source: core/src/mindustry/content/Items.java
# Upstream commit: 2bdedfed25932ebf11330d3584092c50e2e26d96
# Do not edit by hand.
```

### 4. Compatibility Runtime

Runtime code is handwritten GDScript that mimics enough Arc/Mindustry APIs for generated code to load.

Initial runtime files:

- `godot/src/runtime/Seq.gd`
- `godot/src/runtime/ObjectMap.gd`
- `godot/src/runtime/Mathf.gd`
- `godot/src/runtime/Time.gd`
- `godot/src/runtime/Events.gd`
- `godot/src/runtime/Core.gd`
- `godot/src/runtime/Vars.gd`
- `godot/src/runtime/ColorCompat.gd`
- `godot/src/runtime/Vec2.gd`

### 5. Content Runtime

Mindustry content classes need lightweight base models before full gameplay exists.

Initial content base:

- `Content.gd`
- `ContentType.gd`
- `UnlockableContent.gd`
- `Item.gd`
- `Liquid.gd`
- `Block.gd`
- `UnitType.gd`
- `BulletType.gd`

These classes only need enough behavior to instantiate generated content safely in milestone 1.

### 6. Asset Bridge

The bridge copies supported assets from upstream into the Godot project and writes a registry file.

Supported first:

- `.png`
- `.ogg`
- `.woff`
- `.ttf`
- `.frag`
- `.vert`

The bridge must preserve relative source path in metadata so each Godot asset can be traced back.

## Output Layout

```text
C:\Users\Admin\Documents\Game
  mindustry-upstream\
  godot-mindustry\
    project.godot
    addons\
    assets\
    src\
      runtime\
      mindustry\
      generated\
  tools\
    mindustry_godot_transpiler\
      scanner\
      parser\
      emitter\
      reports\
      fixtures\
  docs\
    superpowers\
      specs\
      plans\
```

## Milestones

| Milestone | Goal | Done criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| M1 | Loadable Godot project with generated content subset | Godot opens project, generated files parse, report shows unsupported constructs |
| M2 | Broader content generation | `Items`, `Liquids`, selected `Blocks`, selected `Bullets` instantiate |
| M3 | Map/world data structures | Tiles, world grid, block placement data model loads |
| M4 | Minimal simulation runtime | Drill, conveyor, item storage, core inventory update in Godot |
| M5 | Visual asset rendering | Generated content references sprites and sounds through registry |
| M6 | Continuous regression suite | Fixture and Godot parse tests run from one command |

## Risks

| Risk | Control |
| --- | --- |
| Generated `mindustry.gen` code missing from source | Build or stub generated API explicitly; record every missing generated dependency |
| Java semantics do not map cleanly to GDScript | Use compatibility runtime and unsupported-feature reports |
| Static initialization order breaks content | Add deterministic registry load order and tests around `Items`, `Liquids`, `Blocks` |
| Overloads conflict in GDScript | Generate unique names plus dispatchers where required |
| Arc/LibGDX APIs are too broad | Implement only APIs reached by selected milestone source files |
| GPL compliance missed | Copy license and source notice into generated project before distribution |

## Verification Plan

Every phase must produce terminal evidence.

Required checks:

```powershell
git -C mindustry-upstream rev-parse HEAD
godot --headless --path godot-mindustry --quit
python tools/mindustry_godot_transpiler/transpile.py scan
python tools/mindustry_godot_transpiler/transpile.py emit --subset content-core
python tools/mindustry_godot_transpiler/transpile.py report
```

When Godot CLI is not on `PATH`, use the configured MCP Godot path from `C:\Users\Admin\.codex\config.toml`.
